(function(){function show(m){var b=document.getElementById('errBar');if(!b){b=document.createElement('div');b.id='errBar';
b.style.cssText='position:fixed;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;z-index:99999;background:#b91c1c;color:#fff;padding:10px;white-space:pre-wrap;font:13px monospace';

<script>
/* ===== O-NET PDF parser (สทศ.) — อาศัยตัวเลข/ตำแหน่งแถวเป็นหลัก เพราะตัวอักษรไทยใน PDF ของ สทศ. เพี้ยนเวลาดึงข้อความ ===== */
const OnetParser = (function(){
  const LV7 = ['sch','size','loc','prov','dept','reg','nat'];
  const LV11 = ['sch','size','locIn','locOut','prov','area','dept','moeReg','reg','moi','nat'];
  const SUBJ = {'91':'ภาษาไทย','92':'สังคมศึกษา ศาสนา และวัฒนธรรม','93':'ภาษาอังกฤษ','94':'คณิตศาสตร์','95':'วิทยาศาสตร์','01':'ภาษาไทย','02':'สังคมศึกษา ศาสนา และวัฒนธรรม','03':'ภาษาอังกฤษ','04':'คณิตศาสตร์','05':'วิทยาศาสตร์'};
  const CANON={'91':'TH','92':'SO','93':'EN','94':'MA','95':'SC','01':'TH','02':'SO','03':'EN','04':'MA','05':'SC'};
  const PFX = {'91':'ท','92':'ส','93':'ต','94':'ค','95':'ว','01':'ท','02':'ส','03':'ต','04':'ค','05':'ว'};
  const FORM_BY_FILE = [[/IndividualScore/i,1],[/ContentStat/i,5],[/SbjStat/i,6],[/ScoreDistribution/i,4],[/StdQuality/i,3],[/StatbySchool/i,2]];

  function toLines(items){
    const its = items.map(i=>({x:i.x,y:i.y,s:String(i.s||'').trim()})).filter(i=>i.s!=='');
    its.sort((a,b)=>b.y-a.y||a.x-b.x);
    const lines=[];
    its.forEach(it=>{ let l=lines.find(l=>Math.abs(l.y-it.y)<=3); if(!l){ l={y:it.y,items:[]}; lines.push(l); } l.items.push(it); });
    lines.sort((a,b)=>b.y-a.y);
    lines.forEach(l=>{ l.items.sort((a,b)=>a.x-b.x); l.text=l.items.map(i=>i.s).join(' '); });
    return lines;
  }
  // คืน token ตัวเลข (raw) ตามลำดับ; "-" = ไม่มีค่า
  function tokens(str){
    const out=[]; const re=/(^|\s)(-?\d{1,3}(?:,\d{3})+(?:\.\d+)?|-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?|-)(?=\s|$)/g; let m;
    str=String(str).replace(/\*/g,' ');
    while((m=re.exec(str))) out.push(m[2]);
    return out;
  }
  const val = t => t==='-'||t==null ? null : parseFloat(String(t).replace(/,/g,''));
  function labelOf(line){ // ข้อความก่อนตัวเลขตัวแรก
    const parts=[]; for(const it of line.items){ if(/^-?[\d,]+(\.\d+)?$/.test(it.s)) break; parts.push(it.s); } return parts.join(' ').trim();
  }
  function pack(raw){ // 15 token = แบบ ปรนัย/อัตนัย/รวม ; 7 token = แบบรวมอย่างเดียว
    const v=raw.map(val);
    if(v.length===15) return {t:[v[0],v[3],v[6],v[9],v[12],v[13],v[14]], pa:[v[1],v[2]]};
    if(v.length===7)  return {t:v.slice(0,7)};
    return null;
  }
  function meta(lines, fileName){
    const head=lines.slice(0,14); const m={};
    const ft = head.map(l=>l.text).join(' \n ').match(/ฉบ\S*\s*(\d)\s*-/);
    m.form = ft ? +ft[1] : null;
    if(!m.form){ for(const [re,f] of FORM_BY_FILE){ if(re.test(fileName||'')){ m.form=f; break; } } }
    for(const l of head){ const c=l.text.match(/(?:^|\s)(\d{10})(?:\s|$)/); if(c){ m.code=c[1]; const idx=l.items.findIndex(i=>i.s===c[1]); const rest=l.items.slice(idx+2).map(i=>i.s).join(' ').trim(); m.name=rest; break; } }
    for(const l of head){
      if(/ขนาด/.test(l.items[0]&&l.items[0].s) && tokens(l.text).length===0){ m.size=l.items[1]&&l.items[1].s; m.loc=l.items[3]&&l.items[3].s; }
      if(/^\S*จ\S*ง\S*ห\S*ด$/.test((l.items[0]&&l.items[0].s)||'') && tokens(l.text).length===0){ m.prov=l.items[1]&&l.items[1].s; m.dept=l.items.slice(5).map(i=>i.s).join(' '); }
    }
    for(const l of head){ const y=l.text.match(/(\d)\s+\S+\s+(25\d\d)/); if(y){ m.grade=+y[1]; m.year=+y[2]; m.stage=/ธยม|มธ/.test(l.text)?'M':(/ถม/.test(l.text)?'P':null); break; } }
    const fm=(fileName||'').match(/_([MP])(\d)_/i); if(fm){ if(!m.stage) m.stage=fm[1].toUpperCase(); if(!m.grade) m.grade=+fm[2]; }
    const fy=(fileName||'').match(/_(25\d\d)_/); if(fy&&!m.year) m.year=+fy[1];
    if(!m.stage) m.stage='M';
    m.level=m.stage+m.grade;
    // ปรับค่าที่ตั้ง/ขนาดจากข้อความเพี้ยน
    const sz=m.size||''; m.sizeN = /^เล/.test(sz)?'เล็ก':/^กล/.test(sz)?'กลาง':/พ.{0,2}เศ|ศษ/.test(sz)?'ใหญ่พิเศษ':/^ให/.test(sz)?'ใหญ่':sz;
    const lc=m.loc||''; m.locN = /^นอก/.test(lc)?'นอกเมือง':/^ใน/.test(lc)?'ในเมือง':lc;
    return m;
  }
  function parseStatForm(lines, form, warn){
    const subj={}; let cur=null, a=0, expA=(form===6?11:7);
    const keys = form===6?LV11:LV7;
    lines.forEach(l=>{
      const hd=l.text.match(/\((\d{2})\)\s*$/);
      if(hd && l.items[0] && /^\S{1,5}$/.test(l.items[0].s) && l.items.length<=8 && !/\d\.\d/.test(l.text)){ cur=hd[1]; subj[cur]=subj[cur]||{lv:{},std:{},cont:{}}; a=0; return; }
      if(!cur) return;
      const raw=tokens(l.text);
      const sc=l.text.match(/(?:^|\s)([ก-ฮ])\s+(\d+\.\d+)(?=\s)/);
      if(form!==5 && sc && raw.length>=15){ // แถวมาตรฐาน
        const rest=l.text.replace(sc[0],' ');
        const r=tokens(rest).map(val);
        if(r.length===15){ subj[cur].std[sc[1]+sc[2]]=r; }
        return;
      }
      if(!raw.length) return;
      if(a<expA && raw[0] && !/\./.test(raw[0]) && (raw.length===7||raw.length===15)){ const p=pack(raw); if(p){ subj[cur].lv[keys[a]]=p.t; if(p.pa&&a===0) subj[cur].paSch=p.pa; a++; } return; }
      if(form===5 && a>=expA && raw.length===15 && /\./.test(raw[0])){ const lab=labelOf(l); subj[cur].cont[lab]=raw.map(val); return; }
    });
    Object.keys(subj).forEach(c=>{ const n=Object.keys(subj[c].lv).length; if(n!==expA) warn.push('วิชา '+c+': พบแถวระดับ '+n+' จาก '+expA); });
    return subj;
  }
  function parseDist(lines){
    const out={};
    lines.forEach(l=>{
      const f=l.items[0]&&l.items[0].s; if(!/^\d{2}$/.test(f||'')||l.items[0].x>70) return;
      const t=tokens(l.items.slice(1).map(i=>i.s).join(' ')); if(t.length>=12 && t.slice(0,12).every(x=>/^\d+$/.test(x))) out[f]=t.slice(0,12).map(Number);
    });
    return out;
  }
  function parseIndividual(lines){
    const codes=[]; let rows=[];
    lines.forEach(l=>{
      const m=l.text.match(/^\s*(\d+)\s+(\d{8})\s+(\d{13})\s/);
      if(m){ const rest=l.text.slice(m[0].length); rows.push(tokens(rest).map(val)); }
      else { (l.text.match(/(?:^|\s)((?:0|9)\d)(?=\s|$)/g)||[]).forEach(s=>{ const c=s.trim(); if(rows.length===0 && !codes.includes(c)) codes.push(c); }); }
    });
    if(!rows.length||!codes.length) return null;
    const k=codes.length; const T=rows[0].length;
    const sc=T-k-1; const split=(sc===k+2);
    const res={n:rows.length, subj:{}};
    codes.forEach((c,i)=>{
      const ti = split ? (i===0?2:i+2) : i; // ตำแหน่ง "รวม"
      const li = sc + i;
      const sv=rows.map(r=>r[ti]).filter(v=>v!=null); const lv=rows.map(r=>r[li]).filter(v=>v!=null);
      res.subj[c]={n:sv.length, abs:rows.length-sv.length, m:sv.length?+(sv.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/sv.length).toFixed(2):null, lm:lv.length?+(lv.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/lv.length).toFixed(2):null};
    });
    return res;
  }
  function parse(pages, fileName){
    const lines=[]; pages.forEach(p=>toLines(p).forEach(l=>lines.push(l)));
    const warn=[]; const m=meta(pages.length?toLines(pages[0]):[], fileName);
    const out={file:fileName,form:m.form,school:{code:m.code,name:m.name,size:m.sizeN,loc:m.locN,prov:m.prov},year:m.year,level:m.level,stage:m.stage,warn};
    if(!m.code||!m.year||!m.form){ out.error='ไม่ใช่รายงาน O-NET ตามแบบ สทศ. (หา รหัสโรงเรียน/ปีการศึกษา/ฉบับที่ ไม่พบ)'; return out; }
    if(m.form===2||m.form===5||m.form===6) out.subj=parseStatForm(lines,m.form,warn);
    else if(m.form===4) out.dist=parseDist(lines);
    else if(m.form===1){ out.ind=parseIndividual(lines); if(!out.ind) warn.push('อ่านตารางรายบุคคลไม่สำเร็จ'); }
    else if(m.form===3){ out.skipped='ฉบับที่ 3 เป็นข้อความอธิบายระดับคุณภาพ ไม่มีตัวเลขสำหรับวิเคราะห์ (ข้าม)'; }
    return out;
  }
  return {parse, SUBJ, PFX, CANON, LV7, LV11};
})();
if(typeof module!=='undefined') module.exports=OnetParser;

'use strict';
/* ===================== CORE: utils / store / auth / UI / charts / export / motion ===================== */
const APP_VERSION = '1.3.2';
const CREDIT_DESIGN = 'ออกแบบระบบโดย ดร.ปิยะพันธ์ พิชญ์ประเสริฐ';
const CREDIT_DEV = 'พัฒนาระบบโดย นนทพัทธ์ หิรัญเรือง';
const UPD_CENTER = ['https:', '', 'nonthaphathr.github.io', 'sisaket-edu-analytics', ''].join('/');
const $ = (s, r) => (r || document).querySelector(s);
const $$ = (s, r) => [...(r || document).querySelectorAll(s)];
const esc = s => String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
const fmt = (v, d = 2) => (v == null || isNaN(v)) ? '–' : Number(v).toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: d, maximumFractionDigits: d });
const fmtI = v => (v == null || isNaN(v)) ? '–' : Number(v).toLocaleString('en-US');
const sgn = (v, d = 2) => (v == null || isNaN(v)) ? '–' : (v > 0 ? '+' : '') + fmt(v, d);
const num = v => { const n = parseFloat(String(v).replace(/,/g, '')); return isNaN(n) ? null : n; };
const sum = a => a.reduce((x, y) => x + y, 0);
const avg = a => a.length ? sum(a) / a.length : null;
const wavg = (rows, vf, wf) => { let w = 0, s = 0; rows.forEach(r => { const v = vf(r), x = wf(r); if (v != null && x > 0) { s += v * x; w += x; } }); return w ? s / w : null; };
const uid = () => Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);
const sleep = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
const lsGet = (k, d) => { try { const v = localStorage.getItem(k); return v == null ? d : JSON.parse(v); } catch (e) { return d; } };
const lsSet = (k, v) => { try { localStorage.setItem(k, JSON.stringify(v)); } catch (e) {} };

/* ---------- store ---------- */
let FORM = null; // โหมดลิงก์กรอกเกรดรายโรงเรียน {s,y,g:[index]|null}
let SIM = false; // โหมดจำลองข้อมูล: ทำงานในหน่วยความจำ ไม่เขียนฐานข้อมูลจริง
const MODE = (window.APP_BOOT && typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) ? 'gas' : 'demo';
const Store = { data: (window.APP_BOOT && window.APP_BOOT.data) || {} };
const col = c => Store.data[c] || (Store.data[c] = {});
function dmerge(a, b) {
  const o = Object.assign({}, a);
  Object.keys(b).forEach(k => {
    const x = o[k], y = b[k];
    if (y && typeof y === 'object' && !Array.isArray(y) && x && typeof x === 'object' && !Array.isArray(x)) o[k] = dmerge(x, y); else o[k] = y;
  });
  return o;
}
let AUTH = null; try { AUTH = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('app_auth') || 'null'); } catch (e) {}
// โรงเรียนล็อกอินครั้งเดียว จำไว้ในเครื่องนี้ 12 ชั่วโมง (หรือจนกดออกจากระบบ)
if (!AUTH) { try { const l = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('app_auth_l') || 'null'); if (l && l.a && l.a.role === 'school' && Date.now() - l.t < 43200000) AUTH = l.a; else localStorage.removeItem('app_auth_l'); } catch (e) {} }
const authStr = () => JSON.stringify(AUTH ? { pin: AUTH.pin, sch: AUTH.sch || '' } : {});
const isAdmin = () => SIM || (!!AUTH && AUTH.role === 'admin');
function setAuth(a) { AUTH = a; try { if (a) sessionStorage.setItem('app_auth', JSON.stringify(a)); else sessionStorage.removeItem('app_auth'); } catch (e) {} try { if (a && a.role === 'school' && !SIM) localStorage.setItem('app_auth_l', JSON.stringify({ a, t: Date.now() })); else localStorage.removeItem('app_auth_l'); } catch (e) {} }

function gasCall(fn, ...args) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(r => { try { resolve(typeof r === 'string' ? JSON.parse(r) : r); } catch (e) { resolve(r); } })
      .withFailureHandler(err => {
        const e = new Error(String(err && err.message || err).replace(/^Error:\s*/, ''));
        if (/PIN_INVALID|PIN_LOCKED/.test(e.message)) { setAuth(null); try { renderChrome(); } catch (x) {} }
        e.message = e.message.replace(/^(PIN_\w+|NO_PIN|NOT_ALLOWED):\s*/, '');
        reject(e);
      })[fn](...args);
  });
}
async function commit(ops, onProg) {
  if (MODE === 'gas' && !SIM) {
    const CH = 60;
    for (let i = 0; i < ops.length; i += CH) {
      await gasCall('apiCommit', authStr(), JSON.stringify(ops.slice(i, i + CH)));
      if (onProg) onProg(Math.min(100, Math.round((i + CH) / ops.length * 100)));
    }
  }
  ops.forEach(o => {
    const c = col(o.c);
    if (o.op === 'delete') delete c[o.id];
    else c[o.id] = o.op === 'merge' ? dmerge(c[o.id] || {}, o.data) : o.data;
  });
  if (typeof onDataChanged === 'function') onDataChanged();
}

/* ---------- modal / toast ---------- */
function toast(msg, kind) {
  const d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'ts ' + (kind || ''); d.textContent = msg;
  $('#toast').appendChild(d);
  setTimeout(() => { d.style.transition = 'opacity .3s,transform .3s'; d.style.opacity = '0'; d.style.transform = 'translateY(8px)'; setTimeout(() => d.remove(), 320); }, kind === 'er' ? 5200 : 2600);
}
let _mdResolve = null;
function openModal(html, wide) {
  const md = $('#md'); md.className = 'md' + (wide ? ' wide' : ''); md.innerHTML = html;
  $('#mask').classList.add('on');
  const f = $('input,select,textarea', md); if (f && !/checkbox|file/.test(f.type)) setTimeout(() => f.focus(), 60);
}
function closeModal(v) { $('#mask').classList.remove('on'); const r = _mdResolve; _mdResolve = null; if (r) r(v); }
function ask(html, opts) { // คืน Promise<true|false>
  opts = opts || {};
  return new Promise(res => {
    _mdResolve = res;
    openModal('<h3>' + esc(opts.title || 'ยืนยัน') + '</h3><div>' + html + '</div><div class="act"><button class="btn" onclick="closeModal(false)">' + esc(opts.cancel || 'ยกเลิก') + '</button><button class="btn pri" onclick="closeModal(true)">' + esc(opts.ok || 'ตกลง') + '</button></div>', opts.wide);
  });
}
const alertBox = (msg, title) => new Promise(res => { _mdResolve = res; openModal('<h3>' + esc(title || 'แจ้งเตือน') + '</h3><div>' + msg + '</div><div class="act"><button class="btn pri" onclick="closeModal(true)">ปิด</button></div>'); });
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Escape' && $('#mask').classList.contains('on')) closeModal(false); });
document.addEventListener('mousedown', e => { if (e.target.id === 'mask') closeModal(false); });

function openLogin() {
  return new Promise(res => {
    _mdResolve = res;
    const sch = Object.values(col('schools')).sort((a, b) => (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '', 'th'));
    openModal('<h3>เข้าสู่ระบบเพื่อบันทึก/แก้ไขข้อมูล</h3>' +
      '<div class="row"><div class="grow"><label class="f">สถานะ</label><select id="lgRole" onchange="$(\'#lgSchW\').style.display=this.value===\'school\'?\'block\':\'none\'">' + (FORM ? '' : '<option value="admin">ผู้ดูแล (ศึกษานิเทศก์ / กลุ่มงานนิเทศ)</option>') + '<option value="school">โรงเรียน</option></select></div></div>' +
      '<div id="lgSchW" style="display:' + (FORM ? 'block' : 'none') + ';margin-top:10px"><label class="f">โรงเรียน</label><select id="lgSch" style="width:100%"' + (FORM ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + sch.filter(s => !FORM || s.code === FORM.s).map(s => '<option value="' + esc(s.code) + '">' + esc(s.name) + '</option>').join('') + '</select></div>' +
      '<div style="margin-top:10px"><label class="f">PIN</label><input type="password" id="lgPin" style="width:100%" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="off" onkeydown="if(event.key===\'Enter\')doLogin()"/></div>' +
      '<div id="lgErr" class="small neg" style="margin-top:8px"></div>' +
      '<div class="act"><button class="btn" onclick="closeModal(false)">ยกเลิก</button><button class="btn pri" id="lgGo" onclick="doLogin()">เข้าสู่ระบบ</button></div>');
  });
}
async function doLogin() {
  const role = $('#lgRole').value, pin = $('#lgPin').value.trim(), sch = role === 'school' ? $('#lgSch').value : '';
  if (!pin) { $('#lgErr').textContent = 'กรุณากรอก PIN'; return; }
  if (role === 'school' && !sch) { $('#lgErr').textContent = 'กรุณาเลือกโรงเรียน (ต้องเพิ่มโรงเรียนในระบบก่อน)'; return; }
  const b = $('#lgGo'); b.disabled = true;
  try {
    if (MODE === 'gas') { const a = { pin, sch }; const prev = AUTH; AUTH = a; const r = await gasCall('apiLogin', JSON.stringify(a)); setAuth({ pin, sch, role: r.role }); }
    else setAuth({ pin, sch, role: role });
    closeModal(true); renderChrome(); toast('เข้าสู่ระบบแล้ว', 'ok'); if (typeof render === 'function') render();
  } catch (e) { AUTH = null; $('#lgErr').textContent = e.message; b.disabled = false; }
}
async function needAuth() { if (AUTH || SIM) return true; return !!(await openLogin()) && !!AUTH; }
function logout() { setAuth(null); renderChrome(); toast('ออกจากระบบแล้ว'); render(); }

/* ---------- motion & micro-interactions (GPU only) ---------- */
const REDUCED = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)').matches;
const io = ('IntersectionObserver' in window) ? new IntersectionObserver(es => es.forEach(en => { if (en.isIntersecting) { en.target.classList.add('in'); io.unobserve(en.target); } }), { threshold: .08, rootMargin: '0px 0px -6% 0px' }) : null;
function initMotion(root) {
  root = root || document;
  // stagger การ์ด/แถวหลักให้ทยอยเข้า
  $$('.card,.hero,.tw,.grid>div', root).forEach((el, i) => { if (!el.dataset.rv) { el.dataset.rv = 1; el.classList.add('reveal'); el.style.transitionDelay = Math.min(i, 10) * 45 + 'ms'; } });
  $$('.reveal:not(.in)', root).forEach(el => { if (REDUCED || !io) el.classList.add('in'); else io.observe(el); });
  // นับเลข KPI ขึ้น
  $$('[data-count]', root).forEach(el => {
    if (el.dataset.done) return; el.dataset.done = 1;
    const to = parseFloat(el.dataset.count), d = +(el.dataset.d || 0), pre = el.dataset.pre || '';
    if (REDUCED || isNaN(to)) { el.textContent = pre + fmt(to, d); return; }
    const t0 = performance.now(), dur = 800;
    (function step(t) { const p = Math.min(1, (t - t0) / dur), e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 3); el.textContent = pre + fmt(to * e, d); if (p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step); })(t0);
  });
}
// ripple เบา ๆ บนปุ่ม
document.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
  const b = e.target.closest('.btn,.tab'); if (!b || REDUCED) return;
  const r = b.getBoundingClientRect(), s = Math.max(r.width, r.height), d = document.createElement('span');
  d.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;border-radius:50%;pointer-events:none;background:currentColor;opacity:.16;transform:scale(0);transition:transform .5s,opacity .6s;width:' + s + 'px;height:' + s + 'px;left:' + (e.clientX - r.left - s / 2) + 'px;top:' + (e.clientY - r.top - s / 2) + 'px';
  if (getComputedStyle(b).position === 'static') b.style.position = 'relative';
  b.style.overflow = 'hidden'; b.appendChild(d);
  requestAnimationFrame(() => { d.style.transform = 'scale(2.4)'; d.style.opacity = '0'; });
  setTimeout(() => d.remove(), 650);
});
// การ์ดเอียงนิด ๆ ตามเมาส์ (เฉพาะ KPI, อุปกรณ์ที่ hover ได้)
function initTilt(root) {
  if (REDUCED || !matchMedia('(hover:hover)').matches) return;
  $$('.kpi', root).forEach(c => {
    if (c.dataset.tilt) return; c.dataset.tilt = 1; let raf = null;
    c.addEventListener('pointermove', e => { const r = c.getBoundingClientRect(), x = (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width, y = (e.clientY - r.top) / r.height; if (raf) return; raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => { raf = null; c.style.transform = 'perspective(800px) rotateX(' + ((.5 - y) * 5) + 'deg) rotateY(' + ((x - .5) * 6) + 'deg) translateY(-2px)'; }); });
    c.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => { c.style.transform = ''; });
    c.style.transition = 'transform .25s ease';
  });
}
function pageIn(el) { if (REDUCED) return; el.style.opacity = '0'; el.style.transform = 'translateY(10px)'; requestAnimationFrame(() => { el.style.transition = 'opacity .35s ease,transform .35s cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1)'; el.style.opacity = '1'; el.style.transform = 'none'; setTimeout(() => { el.style.transition = ''; el.style.transform = ''; }, 400); }); }

/* ---------- charts (SVG ล้วน ไม่พึ่งไลบรารี) ---------- */
const PAL = ['#dc2626', '#f59e0b', '#64748b', '#0ea5e9', '#16a34a', '#a855f7'];
function niceMax(v) { if (!(v > 0)) return 1; const p = Math.pow(10, Math.floor(Math.log10(v))), f = v / p; return (f <= 1 ? 1 : f <= 2 ? 2 : f <= 2.5 ? 2.5 : f <= 5 ? 5 : 10) * p; }
function chartBars(labels, series, o) {
  o = o || {}; const W = 680, H = o.h || 240, L = 44, R = 12, T = 18, B = 46, pw = W - L - R, ph = H - T - B;
  const all = series.flatMap(s => s.vals).filter(v => v != null);
  const mx = o.max || niceMax(Math.max(...all, 0.0001)), n = labels.length, g = pw / Math.max(n, 1), k = series.length, bw = Math.min(34, (g * .74) / k);
  let s = '<svg viewBox="0 0 ' + W + ' ' + H + '" role="img">';
  for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) { const y = T + ph - ph * i / 4; s += '<line x1="' + L + '" x2="' + (W - R) + '" y1="' + y + '" y2="' + y + '" stroke="#f1e5e5"/><text x="' + (L - 6) + '" y="' + (y + 4) + '" font-size="11" text-anchor="end" fill="#8a8f98">' + fmt(mx * i / 4, mx <= 5 ? 1 : 0) + '</text>'; }
  labels.forEach((lb, i) => {
    const cx = L + g * i + g / 2;
    series.forEach((se, j) => {
      const v = se.vals[i]; if (v == null) return; const h = Math.max(0, ph * v / mx), x = cx - (k * bw) / 2 + j * bw, y = T + ph - h;
      s += '<rect x="' + x + '" y="' + y + '" width="' + (bw - 3) + '" height="' + h + '" rx="5" fill="' + (se.color || PAL[j % PAL.length]) + '" style="transform-origin:' + x + 'px ' + (T + ph) + 'px;animation:barUp .7s cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1) both;animation-delay:' + (i * 40) + 'ms"><title>' + esc(se.name + ': ' + fmt(v)) + '</title></rect>';
      if (o.vals !== false && n * k <= 16) s += '<text x="' + (x + (bw - 3) / 2) + '" y="' + (y - 4) + '" font-size="10.5" text-anchor="middle" fill="#444">' + fmt(v, o.d == null ? 1 : o.d) + '</text>';
    });
    const t = String(lb), t1 = t.length > 14 ? t.slice(0, 13) + '…' : t;
    s += '<text x="' + cx + '" y="' + (H - B + 18) + '" font-size="12" text-anchor="middle" fill="#3a3f48">' + esc(t1) + '</text>';
  });
  if (o.ref != null) { const y = T + ph - ph * o.ref / mx; s += '<line x1="' + L + '" x2="' + (W - R) + '" y1="' + y + '" y2="' + y + '" stroke="#16a34a" stroke-dasharray="5 4"/><text x="' + (W - R) + '" y="' + (y - 4) + '" font-size="11" fill="#16a34a" text-anchor="end">' + esc(o.refLabel || '') + '</text>'; }
  s += '</svg>';
  return '<div class="chart">' + s + legend(series) + '</div>';
}
function legend(series) { return series.length > 1 ? '<div class="legend">' + series.map((x, j) => '<span><i style="background:' + (x.color || PAL[j % PAL.length]) + '"></i>' + esc(x.name) + '</span>').join('') + '</div>' : ''; }
function chartLine(labels, series, o) {
  o = o || {}; const W = 680, H = o.h || 240, L = 46, R = 18, T = 16, B = 38, pw = W - L - R, ph = H - T - B;
  const all = series.flatMap(s => s.vals).filter(v => v != null); if (!all.length) return '<div class="empty">ยังไม่มีข้อมูล</div>';
  let mn = o.min != null ? o.min : Math.min(...all), mx = o.max != null ? o.max : Math.max(...all);
  if (mx === mn) { mx += 1; mn -= 1; } const pad = (mx - mn) * .12; if (o.min == null) mn -= pad; if (o.max == null) mx += pad;
  const n = labels.length, X = i => L + (n === 1 ? pw / 2 : pw * i / (n - 1)), Y = v => T + ph - ph * (v - mn) / (mx - mn);
  let s = '<svg viewBox="0 0 ' + W + ' ' + H + '">';
  for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) { const v = mn + (mx - mn) * i / 4, y = Y(v); s += '<line x1="' + L + '" x2="' + (W - R) + '" y1="' + y + '" y2="' + y + '" stroke="#f1e5e5"/><text x="' + (L - 6) + '" y="' + (y + 4) + '" font-size="11" text-anchor="end" fill="#8a8f98">' + fmt(v, o.d == null ? 1 : o.d) + '</text>'; }
  labels.forEach((lb, i) => { s += '<text x="' + X(i) + '" y="' + (H - 12) + '" font-size="12" text-anchor="middle" fill="#3a3f48">' + esc(lb) + '</text>'; });
  series.forEach((se, j) => {
    const c = se.color || PAL[j % PAL.length], pts = se.vals.map((v, i) => v == null ? null : [X(i), Y(v)]).filter(Boolean);
    if (pts.length > 1) s += '<path d="M' + pts.map(p => p[0] + ' ' + p[1]).join(' L') + '" fill="none" stroke="' + c + '" stroke-width="2.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" ' + (se.dash ? 'stroke-dasharray="6 5"' : 'pathLength="1" style="stroke-dasharray:1;stroke-dashoffset:1;animation:draw 1.1s ease forwards"') + '/>';
    se.vals.forEach((v, i) => { if (v == null) return; s += '<circle cx="' + X(i) + '" cy="' + Y(v) + '" r="4.2" fill="#fff" stroke="' + c + '" stroke-width="2.4"><title>' + esc(se.name + ' ' + labels[i] + ': ' + fmt(v)) + '</title></circle><text x="' + X(i) + '" y="' + (Y(v) - 9) + '" font-size="10.5" text-anchor="middle" fill="#444">' + fmt(v, o.d == null ? 1 : o.d) + '</text>'; });
  });
  return '<div class="chart">' + s + '</svg>' + legend(series) + '</div>';
}
function chartScatter(pts, o) {
  o = o || {}; const W = 680, H = 300, L = 48, R = 16, T = 14, B = 42, pw = W - L - R, ph = H - T - B;
  if (!pts.length) return '<div class="empty">ข้อมูลไม่พอ</div>';
  const xs = pts.map(p => p.x), ys = pts.map(p => p.y); let x0 = Math.min(...xs), x1 = Math.max(...xs), y0 = Math.min(...ys), y1 = Math.max(...ys);
  if (x0 === x1) { x0 -= 1; x1 += 1; } if (y0 === y1) { y0 -= 1; y1 += 1; } const px = (x1 - x0) * .1, py = (y1 - y0) * .12; x0 -= px; x1 += px; y0 -= py; y1 += py;
  const X = v => L + pw * (v - x0) / (x1 - x0), Y = v => T + ph - ph * (v - y0) / (y1 - y0);
  let s = '<svg viewBox="0 0 ' + W + ' ' + H + '">';
  if (o.hx != null && o.hx > x0 && o.hx < x1) s += '<line x1="' + X(o.hx) + '" x2="' + X(o.hx) + '" y1="' + T + '" y2="' + (T + ph) + '" stroke="#e8d5d5" stroke-dasharray="4 4"/>';
  if (y0 < 0 && y1 > 0) s += '<line x1="' + L + '" x2="' + (W - R) + '" y1="' + Y(0) + '" y2="' + Y(0) + '" stroke="#16a34a" stroke-dasharray="5 4"/>';
  s += '<rect x="' + L + '" y="' + T + '" width="' + pw + '" height="' + ph + '" fill="none" stroke="#f1e5e5"/>';
  pts.forEach((p, i) => { s += '<circle cx="' + X(p.x) + '" cy="' + Y(p.y) + '" r="6" fill="' + (p.c || '#dc2626') + '" fill-opacity=".78" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="1.5" style="transform-origin:' + X(p.x) + 'px ' + Y(p.y) + 'px;animation:dot .5s ease both;animation-delay:' + Math.min(i * 18, 500) + 'ms"><title>' + esc(p.t) + '</title></circle>'; });
  s += '<text x="' + (L + pw / 2) + '" y="' + (H - 6) + '" text-anchor="middle" font-size="12" fill="#5b6270">' + esc(o.xl || '') + '</text><text transform="translate(13 ' + (T + ph / 2) + ') rotate(-90)" text-anchor="middle" font-size="12" fill="#5b6270">' + esc(o.yl || '') + '</text>';
  s += '<text x="' + L + '" y="' + (H - 24) + '" font-size="11" fill="#8a8f98">' + fmt(x0, 1) + '</text><text x="' + (W - R) + '" y="' + (H - 24) + '" font-size="11" text-anchor="end" fill="#8a8f98">' + fmt(x1, 1) + '</text>';
  return '<div class="chart">' + s + '</svg></div>';
}
function hbars(rows, o) { // [{label,val,txt,color}]
  o = o || {}; const mx = o.max || Math.max(...rows.map(r => Math.abs(r.val || 0)), 0.0001);
  return rows.map((r, i) => '<div class="hb"><span title="' + esc(r.label) + '" style="overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">' + esc(r.label) + '</span><div class="bar"><i style="width:' + Math.max(2, Math.abs(r.val || 0) / mx * 100) + '%;animation-delay:' + Math.min(i * 30, 400) + 'ms;' + (r.color ? 'background:' + r.color : '') + '"></i></div><span class="v">' + esc(r.txt != null ? r.txt : fmt(r.val)) + '</span></div>').join('');
}
const styleAnim = document.createElement('style');
styleAnim.textContent = '@keyframes barUp{from{transform:scaleY(0)}}@keyframes draw{to{stroke-dashoffset:0}}@keyframes dot{from{transform:scale(0);opacity:0}}';
document.head.appendChild(styleAnim);

/* ---------- ตาราง/ป้าย ---------- */
function tbl(head, rows, o) { // head:[{h,cls}] หรือ string ; rows: array of array (HTML ได้)
  o = o || {};
  return '<div class="tw"><table class="t ' + (o.cls || '') + '"><thead><tr>' + head.map(h => typeof h === 'string' ? '<th>' + h + '</th>' : '<th class="' + (h.c || '') + '">' + h.h + '</th>').join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>' +
    (rows.length ? rows.map(r => '<tr>' + r.map((c, i) => { const h = head[i]; return '<td class="' + ((h && h.c) || '') + '">' + (c == null ? '' : c) + '</td>'; }).join('') + '</tr>').join('') : '<tr><td colspan="' + head.length + '" class="c muted">ไม่มีข้อมูล</td></tr>') + '</tbody></table></div>';
}
const bdg = (t, k) => '<span class="bdg ' + (k || 'n') + '">' + esc(t) + '</span>';
const dfx = d => d == null ? '–' : '<span class="' + (d > 0 ? 'pos' : d < 0 ? 'neg' : '') + '">' + sgn(d) + '</span>';
const empty = (m, act) => '<div class="empty">' + (m || 'ยังไม่มีข้อมูล') + (act ? '<div style="margin-top:12px">' + act + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';

/* ---------- Export: พิมพ์/PDF • Word • CSV (ทุกไฟล์มีเครดิตผู้ออกแบบ/ผู้พัฒนา) ---------- */
function creditLines() { return [CREDIT_DESIGN, CREDIT_DEV]; }
function docTitle() { return (($('#top h2') || {}).textContent || 'รายงาน') + (SIM ? ' [ข้อมูลจำลอง]' : ''); }
function headerHtml() {
  const S = getSettings();
  return '<div class="print-head">' + (orgLogoSrc() ? '<img src="' + esc(orgLogoSrc()) + '"/>' : '') + '<img src="' + esc(logoSrc()) + '"/><div><b>' + esc(S.title) + '</b><span>' + esc(S.org) + ' • ' + esc(docTitle()) + ' • พิมพ์เมื่อ ' + new Date().toLocaleString('th-TH') + '</span></div></div>';
}
function printPage() {
  let h = $('#printHead'); if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = 'printHead'; $('#view').prepend(h); }
  h.innerHTML = headerHtml();
  let f = $('#printFoot'); if (!f) { f = document.createElement('div'); f.id = 'printFoot'; $('#view').appendChild(f); }
  f.className = 'print-foot'; f.innerHTML = creditLines().map(esc).join(' &nbsp;|&nbsp; ') + '<br/>' + esc(getSettings().title) + ' v' + APP_VERSION;
  $$('.reveal').forEach(e => e.classList.add('in'));
  const t0 = document.title; document.title = exportName(); dlRun('กำลังเตรียมเอกสารพิมพ์…', exportName());
  setTimeout(() => { dlHide(); window.print(); document.title = t0; }, 700);
}
/* ---------- แอนิเมชันสถานะการโหลด/ดาวน์โหลด ---------- */
function dlRun(title, sub) {
  let el = $('#dlA'); if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'dlA'; document.body.appendChild(el); }
  clearTimeout(el._t); el.className = 'dla show run';
  el.innerHTML = '<div class="dli"><span class="dlic">⬇</span></div><div class="dlb"><b>' + esc(title) + '</b><small>' + esc(sub || '') + '</small><div class="dlp"><i></i></div></div>';
}
function dlOk(title, sub, ms) {
  const el = $('#dlA'); if (!el) return; clearTimeout(el._t);
  el.className = 'dla show done'; el.innerHTML = '<div class="dli"><span class="dlic">✓</span></div><div class="dlb"><b>' + esc(title) + '</b><small>' + esc(sub || '') + '</small><div class="dlp"><i></i></div></div>';
  el._t = setTimeout(() => { el.className = 'dla'; }, ms || 2600);
}
function dlHide() { const el = $('#dlA'); if (el) { clearTimeout(el._t); el.className = 'dla'; } }
function fileTag() { const sc = S.sch && col('schools')[S.sch]; return sc ? '_' + sc.name : ''; }
function exportName() { return safeName(docTitle() + fileTag()); }
function downloadBlob(name, type, content) {
  dlRun('กำลังเตรียมไฟล์…', name); const t0 = Date.now();
  const b = new Blob([content], { type: type }), a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(b); a.download = name; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click();
  setTimeout(() => { URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href); a.remove(); }, 600);
  setTimeout(() => dlOk('ดาวน์โหลดแล้ว', name), Math.max(0, 900 - (Date.now() - t0)));
}
function safeName(s) { return String(s).replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|\s]+/g, '_').slice(0, 60); }
function exportWord() {
  const v = $('#view').cloneNode(true);
  $$('button,.btn,.tabs,.noprint,select,input,textarea,#printHead,#printFoot,svg', v).forEach(e => { if (e.tagName === 'SELECT') { e.replaceWith(document.createTextNode(e.options[e.selectedIndex] ? e.options[e.selectedIndex].text : '')); } else e.remove(); });
  $$('.reveal', v).forEach(e => e.classList.remove('reveal'));
  $$('.noexp', v).forEach(e => e.remove());
  // กลุ่มการ์ดตัวเลข (.grid) → ตารางแถวเดียว ให้เป็นสัดส่วนใน Word
  $$('.grid', v).forEach(g => {
    const cells = [...g.children]; if (!cells.length) return;
    const w = Math.floor(100 / Math.min(cells.length, 4)), tb = document.createElement('table'); tb.setAttribute('width', '100%'); tb.style.cssText = 'border-collapse:separate;border-spacing:6pt;margin:6pt 0';
    let tr; cells.forEach((c, i) => {
      if (i % 4 === 0) { tr = document.createElement('tr'); tb.appendChild(tr); }
      const td = document.createElement('td'); td.setAttribute('width', w + '%'); td.style.cssText = 'border:1px solid #f3c1c1;background:#fff7f7;padding:8pt 10pt;vertical-align:top';
      if (c.querySelector('.v')) {
        const l = $('.l', c), vv = $('.v', c), ss = $('.s', c);
        td.innerHTML = (l ? '<div style="font-size:12pt;color:#555">' + l.innerHTML + '</div>' : '') + '<div style="font-size:24pt;font-weight:bold;color:#b91c1c;line-height:1.1">' + (vv ? vv.innerHTML : '') + '</div>' + (ss ? '<div style="font-size:11pt;color:#777">' + ss.innerHTML + '</div>' : '');
      } else td.innerHTML = c.innerHTML;
      tr.appendChild(td);
    });
    g.replaceWith(tb);
  });
  $$('.hero', v).forEach(h => { const tb = document.createElement('table'); tb.setAttribute('width', '100%'); tb.style.cssText = 'border-collapse:collapse;margin:4pt 0 8pt'; const td = document.createElement('td'); td.style.cssText = 'border:0;border-left:5px solid #dc2626;background:#fde8e8;padding:8pt 12pt'; td.innerHTML = h.innerHTML; const tr = document.createElement('tr'); tr.appendChild(td); tb.appendChild(tr); h.replaceWith(tb); });
  $$('table', v).forEach(t => { const ths = $$('thead th', t); if (ths.length && !ths[ths.length - 1].textContent.trim()) { const n = ths.length - 1; $$('tr', t).forEach(r => { const c = r.children[n]; if (c) c.remove(); }); } });
  const S = getSettings(), css = 'body{font-family:"TH SarabunPSK","Sarabun",Tahoma,sans-serif;font-size:15pt}table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;margin:6pt 0}th,td{border:1px solid #999;padding:3pt 6pt;font-size:13pt}th{background:#fde8e8}h1,h2,h3{color:#991b1b}.card{margin-bottom:10pt}.bdg{font-weight:bold}';
  const html = '<html xmlns:o="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:office" xmlns:w="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:word"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>' + esc(docTitle()) + '</title><style>' + css + '</style></head><body>' +
    '<table style="border:0"><tr><td style="border:0;width:' + (orgLogoSrc() ? 124 : 60) + 'pt">' + (orgLogoSrc() ? '<img src="' + esc(orgLogoSrc()) + '" width="56" height="56"/> ' : '') + '<img src="' + esc(logoSrc()) + '" width="56" height="56"/></td><td style="border:0"><b style="font-size:17pt">' + esc(S.title) + '</b><br/>' + esc(S.org) + ' — ' + esc(docTitle()) + (fileTag() ? '<br/>โรงเรียน: ' + esc(fileTag().slice(1)) : '') + '<br/><small>วันที่ออกรายงาน ' + new Date().toLocaleDateString('th-TH', { dateStyle: 'long' }) + '</small></td></tr></table><hr/>' +
    v.innerHTML + '<hr/><p style="text-align:center;font-size:12pt;color:#555">' + creditLines().map(esc).join('<br/>') + '</p></body></html>';
  downloadBlob(exportName() + '.doc', 'application/msword;charset=utf-8', '﻿' + html);
  toast('ส่งออกไฟล์ Word แล้ว', 'ok');
}
function exportCsv() {
  const tabs = $$('#view table.t'); if (!tabs.length) { toast('หน้านี้ไม่มีตารางให้ส่งออก', 'er'); return; }
  const q = s => '"' + String(s).replace(/"/g, '""').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() + '"';
  let out = [q(getSettings().title), q(docTitle()), q('วันที่ออกรายงาน ' + new Date().toLocaleDateString('th-TH', { dateStyle: 'long' })), ''];
  tabs.forEach((t, i) => {
    const h = t.closest('.card'); const cap = h && $('h3', h); if (cap) out.push(q(cap.textContent));
    $$('tr', t).forEach(r => out.push($$('th,td', r).map(c => q(c.innerText || c.textContent)).join(',')));
    out.push('');
  });
  out.push(q(CREDIT_DESIGN), q(CREDIT_DEV));
  downloadBlob(exportName() + '.csv', 'text/csv;charset=utf-8', '﻿' + out.join('\r\n'));
  toast('ส่งออก CSV แล้ว', 'ok');
}
function exportMenu() {
  return '<span class="noprint" style="display:inline-flex;gap:6px"><button class="btn sm" onclick="printPage()" title="พิมพ์ / บันทึกเป็น PDF">🖨 พิมพ์/PDF</button><button class="btn sm" onclick="exportWord()">📄 Word</button><button class="btn sm" onclick="exportCsv()">📊 CSV</button><button class="btn sm" onclick="go(\'report\')" title="รายงานสรุปทั้งระบบพร้อมปก">📘 รายงาน</button></span>';
}

/* ===================== ANALYSIS: settings / index / aggregates / simulation ===================== */
const DEF = { title: 'ระบบสารสนเทศวิเคราะห์ผลสัมฤทธิ์ทางการเรียนและผลการทดสอบระดับชาติ', short: 'Sisaket PAO Education Analytics', org: 'องค์การบริหารส่วนจังหวัดศรีสะเกษ', total: 39, band: 1, accHi: 2.9, accMid: 2.5, stdA: 50, stdB: 60, stdC: 75, hiAcad: 3.0, logo: '', labelMap: {} };
const getSettings = () => Object.assign({}, DEF, col('settings').app || {});
const DEFAULT_LOGO = 'data:image/svg+xml;utf8,' + encodeURIComponent('<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 96 96"><defs><linearGradient id="g" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#fb7185"/><stop offset=".55" stop-color="#dc2626"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#9f1239"/></linearGradient></defs><rect width="96" height="96" rx="22" fill="url(#g)"/><g fill="#fff"><rect x="21" y="52" width="12" height="23" rx="3" opacity=".85"/><rect x="42" y="38" width="12" height="37" rx="3"/><rect x="63" y="22" width="12" height="53" rx="3" opacity=".92"/></g><path d="M20 38 L40 26 L56 32 L76 14" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="4" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity=".9"/></svg>');
const logoSrc = () => getSettings().logo || DEFAULT_LOGO;
const orgLogoSrc = () => getSettings().orgLogo || '';
const fixLabel = s => (getSettings().labelMap || {})[s] || s;

const SUBJ_NAME = { TH: 'ภาษาไทย', MA: 'คณิตศาสตร์', SC: 'วิทยาศาสตร์', EN: 'ภาษาอังกฤษ', SO: 'สังคมศึกษาฯ' };
const SUBJ_ORDER = ['TH', 'MA', 'SC', 'EN', 'SO'];
const GROUPS = [{ k: 'ภาษาไทย', cn: 'TH' }, { k: 'คณิตศาสตร์', cn: 'MA' }, { k: 'วิทยาศาสตร์และเทคโนโลยี', cn: 'SC' }, { k: 'สังคมศึกษา ศาสนาและวัฒนธรรม', cn: 'SO' }, { k: 'สุขศึกษาและพลศึกษา' }, { k: 'ศิลปะ' }, { k: 'การงานอาชีพ' }, { k: 'ภาษาต่างประเทศ', cn: 'EN' }];
const GRADES = [0, 1, 1.5, 2, 2.5, 3, 3.5, 4];
const LEVEL_NAME = { M3: 'ม.3', M6: 'ม.6', P6: 'ป.6' };
const sName = c => { const s = col('schools')[c]; return s ? fixLabel(s.name || c) : String(c); };

function colorOf(d) { const b = getSettings().band; return d == null ? { k: 'n', t: '–' } : d >= b ? { k: 'g', t: 'สูงกว่าประเทศ' } : d <= -b ? { k: 'r', t: 'ต่ำกว่าประเทศ' } : { k: 'y', t: 'ใกล้เคียง' }; }
function accLabel(m) { const S = getSettings(); return m == null ? ['–', 'n'] : m >= S.accHi ? ['จุดแข็ง', 'g'] : m >= S.accMid ? ['ควรพัฒนา', 'y'] : ['เร่งพัฒนา', 'r']; }
function stdLabel(p) { const S = getSettings(); return p == null ? ['–', 'n'] : p < S.stdA ? ['เร่งพัฒนา', 'r'] : p < S.stdB ? ['ควรพัฒนา', 'y'] : p < S.stdC ? ['ปานกลาง', 'b'] : ['ดี', 'g']; }
const lbBdg = a => bdg(a[0], a[1]);
const colBdg = d => { const c = colorOf(d); return bdg(c.t, c.k); };

/* ---------- ดัชนีข้อมูล ---------- */
let IDX = null;
function onDataChanged() { IDX = null; }
function acadStat(r) {
  if (r.c && r.c.length === 8) { const n = sum(r.c); return { n, mean: n ? sum(r.c.map((x, i) => x * GRADES[i])) / n : null, pass: n ? (n - r.c[0]) / n * 100 : null, fail: r.c[0], p3: n ? (r.c[5] + r.c[6] + r.c[7]) / n * 100 : null }; }
  const n = r.n || 0; return { n, mean: r.mean, pass: r.pass, fail: (r.pass != null && n) ? Math.round(n * (100 - r.pass) / 100) : null, p3: r.p3 };
}
function idx() {
  if (IDX) return IDX;
  const onet = Object.values(col('onet')).map(r => Object.assign({ cn: OnetParser.CANON[r.c] || r.c }, r));
  const acad = Object.values(col('acad')).map(r => Object.assign({ st: acadStat(r) }, r));
  const years = [...new Set(onet.map(r => +r.y).concat(acad.map(r => +r.y)))].filter(Boolean).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const levels = [...new Set(onet.map(r => r.l))].sort();
  IDX = { onet, acad, years, levels, schools: Object.values(col('schools')).sort((a, b) => sName(a.code).localeCompare(sName(b.code), 'th')) };
  return IDX;
}
const latestYear = () => { const y = idx().years; return y.length ? y[y.length - 1] : null; };
const yearsOf = (l) => [...new Set(idx().onet.filter(r => !l || r.l === l).map(r => +r.y))].sort((a, b) => a - b);

/* ---------- O-NET aggregates ---------- */
function subjRecs(y, l, cn) { return idx().onet.filter(r => +r.y === +y && r.l === l && r.cn === cn && r.lv && r.lv.sch && r.lv.sch[1] != null); }
function aggSubject(y, l, cn) {
  const rs = subjRecs(y, l, cn); if (!rs.length) return null;
  const ref = k => { const r = rs.find(r => r.lv[k] && r.lv[k][1] != null); return r ? r.lv[k][1] : null; };
  const rows = rs.map(r => { const m = r.lv.sch[1], nt = r.lv.nat ? r.lv.nat[1] : null; return { s: r.s, name: sName(r.s), n: r.lv.sch[0] || 0, m, nat: nt, prov: r.lv.prov ? r.lv.prov[1] : null, dept: r.lv.dept ? r.lv.dept[1] : null, diff: (m != null && nt != null) ? m - nt : null, rec: r }; });
  const mean = wavg(rows, r => r.m, r => r.n), nat = ref('nat');
  const cnt = { g: 0, y: 0, r: 0 }; rows.forEach(r => { if (r.diff != null) cnt[colorOf(r.diff).k]++; });
  return { cn, n: sum(rows.map(r => r.n)), mean, nat, prov: ref('prov'), dept: ref('dept'), diff: (mean != null && nat != null) ? mean - nat : null, rows, cnt, schools: rows.length };
}
const stdSort = (a, b) => { const f = s => { const m = s.match(/(\d+)\.(\d+)/); return m ? +m[1] * 100 + +m[2] : 0; }; return f(a) - f(b); };
function stdAgg(y, l, cn) {
  const map = {};
  subjRecs(y, l, cn).filter(r => r.std).forEach(r => Object.keys(r.std).forEach(k => { const a = r.std[k]; if (a[1] == null || a[13] == null) return; (map[k] = map[k] || []).push({ s: r.s, w: r.lv.sch[0] || 1, m: a[1], nat: a[13], prov: a[7], diff: a[1] - a[13] }); }));
  return Object.keys(map).sort(stdSort).map(k => { const L = map[k]; return { k, rows: L, m: wavg(L, x => x.m, x => x.w), nat: avg(L.map(x => x.nat)), prov: avg(L.map(x => x.prov)), diff: wavg(L, x => x.diff, x => x.w), below: L.filter(x => x.diff < 0).length, n: L.length }; });
}
function schoolStd(y, l, cn, s) { const r = idx().onet.find(r => +r.y === +y && r.l === l && r.cn === cn && r.s === s); if (!r || !r.std) return []; return Object.keys(r.std).sort(stdSort).map(k => { const a = r.std[k]; return { k, m: a[1], nat: a[13], prov: a[7], diff: (a[1] != null && a[13] != null) ? a[1] - a[13] : null }; }); }
function schoolRec(y, l, cn, s) { return idx().onet.find(r => +r.y === +y && r.l === l && r.cn === cn && r.s === s); }
function trendOf(s, l, cn) { // s=null → ทั้งสังกัด
  return yearsOf(l).map(y => {
    if (s) { const r = schoolRec(y, l, cn, s); if (!r || !r.lv.sch) return { y, m: null, nat: null, diff: null }; const m = r.lv.sch[1], nt = r.lv.nat ? r.lv.nat[1] : null; return { y, m, nat: nt, diff: (m != null && nt != null) ? m - nt : null, n: r.lv.sch[0] }; }
    const a = aggSubject(y, l, cn); return a ? { y, m: a.mean, nat: a.nat, diff: a.diff, n: a.n } : { y, m: null, nat: null, diff: null };
  });
}
function trendFlags(tr) {
  const v = tr.filter(t => t.m != null), f = [];
  if (v.length >= 2) { const d = v[v.length - 1].m - v[v.length - 2].m; f.push(Math.abs(d) < .5 ? ['คะแนนคงที่', 'y'] : d > 0 ? ['คะแนนเพิ่มขึ้น', 'g'] : ['คะแนนลดลง', 'r']); }
  const dd = v.filter(t => t.diff != null);
  if (dd.length >= 2 && dd.every(t => t.diff > 0)) f.push(['สูงกว่าระดับประเทศต่อเนื่อง', 'g']);
  if (dd.length >= 2 && dd.every(t => t.diff < 0)) f.push(['ต่ำกว่าระดับประเทศต่อเนื่อง', 'r']);
  return f;
}

/* ---------- ผลสัมฤทธิ์ทางการเรียน ---------- */
function acadRows(y, o) { o = o || {}; return idx().acad.filter(r => (y == null || +r.y === +y) && (!o.s || r.s === o.s) && (!o.g || r.g === o.g)); }
function acadAgg(rows) {
  const w = rows.filter(r => r.st && r.st.n > 0 && r.st.mean != null); if (!w.length) return null;
  const n = sum(w.map(r => r.st.n));
  return { n, mean: wavg(w, r => r.st.mean, r => r.st.n), pass: wavg(w, r => r.st.pass, r => r.st.n), p3: wavg(w, r => r.st.p3, r => r.st.n), fail: sum(w.map(r => r.st.fail || 0)), schools: new Set(w.map(r => r.s)).size };
}
const groupAgg = (y, g) => acadAgg(acadRows(y, { g }));
function submittedSchools(y) { const s = new Set(); idx().onet.forEach(r => { if (+r.y === +y) s.add(r.s); }); idx().acad.forEach(r => { if (+r.y === +y) s.add(r.s); }); return s; }
// ภาพรวมรายโรงเรียน
function schoolProfile(y, l, s) {
  const out = { s, subj: {}, strengths: [], weak: [] };
  SUBJ_ORDER.forEach(cn => { const r = schoolRec(y, l, cn, s); if (r && r.lv.sch && r.lv.nat) out.subj[cn] = { m: r.lv.sch[1], nat: r.lv.nat[1], diff: r.lv.sch[1] - r.lv.nat[1] }; });
  const e = Object.keys(out.subj).sort((a, b) => out.subj[b].diff - out.subj[a].diff);
  out.best = e[0] || null; out.worst = e.length > 1 ? e[e.length - 1] : null;
  out.below = e.filter(k => out.subj[k].diff <= -getSettings().band).length; out.above = e.filter(k => out.subj[k].diff >= getSettings().band).length;
  out.totalDiff = e.length ? sum(e.map(k => out.subj[k].diff)) : null;
  return out;
}
function pearson(a, b) { const n = a.length; if (n < 3) return null; const ma = avg(a), mb = avg(b); let sab = 0, saa = 0, sbb = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { sab += (a[i] - ma) * (b[i] - mb); saa += (a[i] - ma) ** 2; sbb += (b[i] - mb) ** 2; } return saa && sbb ? sab / Math.sqrt(saa * sbb) : null; }

function mulberry(a) { return function () { a |= 0; a = a + 0x6D2B79F5 | 0; let t = Math.imul(a ^ a >>> 15, 1 | a); t = t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, 61 | t) ^ t; return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296; }; }
function makeSim(seed) {
  const R = mulberry(seed || 20260101), G = (m, s) => { let u = 0, v = 0; while (!u) u = R(); v = R(); return m + s * Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(u)) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * v); };
  const clip = (v, a, b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v)), r2 = v => Math.round(v * 100) / 100;
  const names = ['กระดุมทองวิทยา', 'ผักแพววิทยา', 'กันทรอมวิทยาคม', 'บึงบูรพ์วิทยา', 'ขุนหาญวิทยาคม', 'ไพรบึงวิทยา', 'ภูสิงห์วิทยา', 'ห้วยทับทันวิทยา', 'ราษีไศลวิทยาคม', 'อุทุมพรพิสัยวิทยา', 'ศรีรัตนะวิทยา', 'โนนคูณวิทยา', 'พยุห์วิทยาคม', 'ยางชุมน้อยวิทยา', 'เมืองจันทร์วิทยา', 'วังหินวิทยาคม', 'น้ำเกลี้ยงวิทยา', 'เบญจลักษ์วิทยา', 'ปรางค์กู่วิทยา', 'ศิลาลาดวิทยา', 'โพธิ์ศรีสุวรรณวิทยา', 'กันทรลักษ์วิทยาคม', 'ทุ่งไชยวิทยา', 'ลำภูวิทยา', 'หนองหญ้าลาดวิทยา', 'นาโพธิ์วิทยา', 'ดงรัตน์วิทยา', 'บ้านแทรงวิทยา', 'จานแสนไชยวิทยา', 'ดูนสาดวิทยา', 'ตูมใหญ่วิทยา', 'พิมายวิทยานุสรณ์', 'สร้างปี่วิทยา', 'ก้านเหลืองวิทยา', 'หัวช้างวิทยา', 'ละลมวิทยา', 'ตระการวิทยา', 'ขนุนวิทยา', 'ทุ่งสว่างวิทยา'];
  const out = { schools: {}, onet: {}, acad: {}, improve: {}, settings: {} };
  const years = [2566, 2567, 2568];
  const yOff = {}; years.forEach(y => { yOff[y] = {}; ['TH', 'MA', 'SC', 'EN', 'SO'].forEach(cn => yOff[y][cn] = y === 2568 ? 0 : G(0.4 * (2568 - y), 1.1)); });
  const subjByLevel = { M3: ['TH', 'MA', 'SC', 'EN'], M6: ['TH', 'SO', 'EN', 'MA', 'SC'] };
  const code2 = { M3: { TH: '91', MA: '94', SC: '95', EN: '93', SO: '92' }, M6: { TH: '01', MA: '04', SC: '05', EN: '03', SO: '02' } };
  const gAcad = { 'ภาษาไทย': 2.85, 'คณิตศาสตร์': 2.45, 'วิทยาศาสตร์และเทคโนโลยี': 2.9, 'สังคมศึกษา ศาสนาและวัฒนธรรม': 3.05, 'สุขศึกษาและพลศึกษา': 3.25, 'ศิลปะ': 3.3, 'การงานอาชีพ': 3.15, 'ภาษาต่างประเทศ': 2.6 };
  const cdf = z => { const t = 1 / (1 + .2316419 * Math.abs(z)), d = .3989423 * Math.exp(-z * z / 2), p = d * t * (.3193815 + t * (-.3565638 + t * (1.781478 + t * (-1.821256 + t * 1.330274)))); return z > 0 ? 1 - p : p; };
  names.forEach((nm, i) => {
    const code = '30331001' + String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0'), size = i % 5 === 0 ? 'กลาง' : i % 9 === 0 ? 'ใหญ่' : 'เล็ก';
    const stu = Math.round(size === 'ใหญ่' ? G(900, 150) : size === 'กลาง' ? G(500, 90) : G(210, 70)), stuC = clip(stu, 60, 1500);
    out.schools[code] = { code, name: 'โรงเรียน' + nm, size, loc: i % 4 === 0 ? 'ในเมือง' : 'นอกเมือง', students: stuC, teachers: Math.round(stuC / 17 + G(2, 1)), levels: 'ม.1–ม.6' };
    const eS = G(-1.4, 3.1), eSub = {}, tr = G(0.15, 0.7), acEff = 0.035 * eS + G(0, 0.2);
    ['TH', 'MA', 'SC', 'EN', 'SO'].forEach(cn => eSub[cn] = G(0, 2.1));
    ['M3', 'M6'].forEach(l => {
      const nn = Math.round(clip(stuC / (l === 'M3' ? 3.3 : 4.4) * (l === 'M6' ? .8 : 1), 12, 260));
      years.forEach(y => subjByLevel[l].forEach(cn => {
        const ref = (DEMO_REF[l] || {})[cn]; if (!ref) return;
        const off = yOff[y][cn], key = code + '|' + y + '|' + l + '|' + code2[l][cn];
        const natM = ref.lv.nat[1] + off, mk = k => { const a = ref.lv[k].slice(); a[1] = r2(a[1] + off + (k === 'size' ? G(0, .4) : 0)); return a; };
        const sm = clip(natM + eS * 1 + eSub[cn] + tr * (y - 2568) + G(0, 1.3), 4, 92), sd = r2(ref.lv.nat[2] * .78 * clip(G(1, .12), .6, 1.3));
        const sch = [nn, r2(sm), sd, r2(clip(sm + sd * 2.4, 10, 100)), r2(clip(sm - sd * 2.2, 0, 60)), r2(sm - G(.6, .5)), null];
        const lv = { sch, size: mk('size'), loc: mk('loc'), prov: mk('prov'), dept: mk('dept'), reg: mk('reg'), nat: mk('nat') };
        const rec = { s: code, y, l, c: code2[l][cn], lv };
        if (ref.std) { rec.std = {}; Object.keys(ref.std).forEach(k => { const b = ref.std[k], nt = b[13] + off, v = clip(nt + eS * .9 + eSub[cn] * .9 + G(0, 4.5), 3, 96), sdv = r2(clip(b[2] * 1.1 + G(0, 3), 8, 38)); const arr = b.slice(); arr[1] = r2(v); arr[2] = sdv; [3, 5, 7, 9, 11, 13].forEach(ix => arr[ix] = r2(b[ix] + off + G(0, .25))); rec.std[k] = arr; }); }
        const bins = []; for (let b = 0; b < 10; b++) bins.push(Math.max(0, Math.round(nn * (cdf((10 * (b + 1) - sm) / sd) - cdf((10 * b - sm) / sd))))); bins.push(0);
        const diff = nn - sum(bins); const mi = bins.indexOf(Math.max(...bins)); bins[mi] += diff; bins.push(nn); rec.dist = bins;
        out.onet[key] = rec;
      }));
    });
    years.forEach(y => GROUPS.forEach(g => {
      const mu = clip(gAcad[g.k] + acEff + 0.03 * (y - 2568) + G(0, .12) + (g.cn && g.cn !== 'SO' ? 0.02 * eSub[g.cn] : 0), 1.6, 3.8), n = clip(stuC + Math.round(G(0, 8)), 40, 1600);
      const w = GRADES.map(gr => Math.exp(-((gr - mu) ** 2) / (2 * .85 * .85)) * (gr === 0 ? .55 : 1)), tw = sum(w), raw = w.map(x => x / tw * n), c = raw.map(Math.floor); let rem = n - sum(c);
      raw.map((x, ix) => [x - Math.floor(x), ix]).sort((a, b) => b[0] - a[0]).forEach(p => { if (rem > 0) { c[p[1]]++; rem--; } });
      out.acad[code + '|' + y + '|' + g.k] = { s: code, y, g: g.k, c };
    }));
  });
  const L = Object.keys(out.schools), pick = i => L[i % L.length];
  [['การอ่านและการคิดคำนวณต่ำกว่าค่าเฉลี่ยประเทศ', 'อบรมครูด้านการจัดการเรียนรู้เพื่อส่งเสริมการอ่าน', 'อบรม', 'นิเทศชั้นเรียน', 'ดำเนินการ', 24, 18], ['คะแนนด้านการคิดคำนวณต่ำ', 'พัฒนาการออกแบบกิจกรรมแก้ปัญหาทางคณิตศาสตร์', 'โค้ช', 'ตรวจแผนและสังเกตชั้นเรียน', 'ดำเนินการ', 12, 9], ['คะแนนด้านการคิดวิเคราะห์ต่ำ', 'พัฒนาการจัดการเรียนรู้แบบ Active Learning', 'อบรม', 'สังเกตพฤติกรรมผู้เรียน', 'วางแผน', 0, 0], ['ผลสัมฤทธิ์ไม่พัฒนาต่อเนื่อง', 'จัดทำ PLC วิเคราะห์ปัญหารายโรงเรียน', 'PLC', 'ติดตามคะแนนรายภาคเรียน', 'ติดตามผล', 8, 6]].forEach((r, i) => {
    const id = 'sim' + i; out.improve[id] = { id, s: i === 0 ? '' : pick(i * 7 + 3), issue: r[0], plan: r[1], method: r[2], track: r[3], status: r[4], teachers: r[5], plans: r[6], outcome: i === 3 ? 'คะแนนเฉลี่ยวิชาคณิตศาสตร์เพิ่มขึ้นเล็กน้อยเมื่อเทียบกับปีก่อน' : '', baseline: '', current: '', date: '2568-06-0' + (i + 1) };
  });
  out.settings.app = { total: 39 };
  return out;
}
let REAL_DATA = null;
function setSim(on) {
  if (on === SIM) return;
  if (on) { REAL_DATA = Store.data; Store.data = makeSim(); SIM = true; try { sessionStorage.setItem('app_sim', '1'); } catch (e) {} }
  else { Store.data = REAL_DATA || ((window.APP_BOOT && window.APP_BOOT.data) || {}); REAL_DATA = null; SIM = false; try { sessionStorage.removeItem('app_sim'); } catch (e) {} }
  IDX = null; S.year = null; renderChrome(); render();
  toast(on ? 'เปิดโหมดจำลองข้อมูลแล้ว (ไม่กระทบข้อมูลจริง)' : 'กลับสู่ข้อมูลจริงแล้ว', 'ok');
}
